(모드2) 초안 검수 — 논리 정합성·근거 충실성·정책 타당성·정량성·한국어 교열을 점검하고 위치·문제·해결을 갖춘 수정 요청을 작성. 정책연구 설계나 보고서 초안을 검토·검수·점검하거나 연구설계 게이트를 통과시킬 때 반드시 사용. 막연한 감상이 아닌 구조적·근거 기반 검수에 적용. ([parkjui92/policy-research-kit](https://github.com/parkjui92/policy-research-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/policy-report-review) · ★10)
- **policy-report-writing**: 정책연구보고서 본문을 집필하는 스킬. 승인된 연구설계(목차·분석틀)와 출처 있는 근거를 종합해 현황·실태 분석→문제·쟁점 도출→정책대안 비교→정책제언·결론으로 이어지는 논증형 본문을 쓴다. 정책연구보고서·정책분석 보고서의 본문을 집필하거나 현황분석·정책대안·정책제언을 작성할 때 반드시 사용. 평가 득점이 아닌 정책적 타당성과 근거 기반 논증에 정조준한 집필에 적용. ([parkjui92/policy-research-kit](https://github.com/parkjui92/policy-research-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/policy-report-writing) · ★10)
- **policy-representation**: Draft a policy-led representation on a UK planning application — in **support** or in **objection**, as the user chooses — from a policy compliance assessment. Each point is anchored to a quoted adopted development-plan policy and to the application's own documents, the other side is acknowledged, and the ask is clear (grant, grant subject to conditions, refuse, or do not determine yet). Will not manufacture policy compliance or conflict to fit a stance, and flags any mismatch between the stance and the evidence. Use for "write a letter of support", "draft an objection based on the policies",… ([SeagullTwo/uk-planning-skills](https://github.com/SeagullTwo/uk-planning-skills/tree/HEAD/policy-representation) · ★4)

- **policy-requirements-explorer**: Input policy document (links or downloads) to generate a flowchart, service blueprint, and plain list of all policy-based delivery requirements. Best used for discovery and team alignment. Examples: - "Looking at Federal Regulation X, Memo Y and State Manual Z, what are the income requirements for AK residents to be eligible for Medicaid?" - "Based on policy in the VA's M21 manual, what are the documentation requirements for a veteran with a presumptive claim who is applying for benefits?" Results should be reviewed by a human! ([navapbc/skills-registry](https://github.com/navapbc/skills-registry/tree/HEAD/enterprise/policy-requirements-explorer) · ★2)
- **policy-research**: 정책연구보고서의 근거를 마련하는 리서치 스킬. 설계자의 목차·연구질문에 따라 정부 통계·공식 지표, 선행연구·문헌, 국내외 정책사례, 법·제도·정책 배경을 외부 웹과 사용자 제공 자료에서 조사하고 모든 사실에 출처를 병기한다. 정책연구의 현황·대안 근거를 수집하거나 통계·선행연구·해외사례·정책배경을 조사할 때 사용. 일반 웹검색이 아닌 정책연구 근거 수집 목적에 적용. ([parkjui92/policy-research-kit](https://github.com/parkjui92/policy-research-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/policy-research) · ★10)
- **policy-research**: Research a jurisdiction's policy or compliance question with official sources, effective dates, applicability, and product implications. ([nesnilnehc/ai-cortex](https://github.com/nesnilnehc/ai-cortex/tree/HEAD/skills/policy-research) · ★8)
- **policy-research**: This skill should be used when the user needs to conduct comprehensive multi-source research on a municipal policy topic. Triggers include any mention of policy research, policy analysis, best practices research, peer community comparison, policy options evaluation, or when the user needs to research a policy question to inform an upcoming decision, long-range planning, or council deliberation. - **policy-research**: Rate a subscription provider's terms of service, privacy policy and other policies on the topics in data/policy-topics.yaml (five grades, great to bad), quoting its own pages word for word, for a card's Terms and policies (data/providers/&lt;id&gt;.yaml, policies). Use when adding a provider, re-reading its terms, or when the price-research skill reads its policies. - **policy-research-design**: 정책연구의 착수 단계를 설계하는 스킬. 본격 조사·집필 전에 (1) 연구 의도·범위·숨은 요구를 분석해 모호성을 걷어내고 사용자 확인 질문을 만들고, (2) 핵심 연구질문(RQ)·분석틀·보고서 목차·연구방법을 설계한다. 정책연구·정책분석을 새로 시작하거나, 주제가 막연해 방향을 잡아야 하거나, 연구설계·목차·분석틀·연구질문을 짤 때 반드시 사용. 단순 글쓰기가 아닌 정책연구 착수 게이트에 적용. ([parkjui92/policy-research-kit](https://github.com/parkjui92/policy-research-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/policy-research-design) · ★10)

- **policy-retrieval**: 检索并核验政府项目从管理办法、当期通知、指南附件、官方答疑、更正延期到公示结果的完整政策链；用户只要求核验政策版本、效力、是否过期、是否被替代或完整文件链时，本技能必须作为主技能，即使问题提到具体项目领域。 ([sakuruamayday-ops/enterprise-project-application-assistant](https://github.com/sakuruamayday-ops/enterprise-project-application-assistant/tree/HEAD/skills/policy-retrieval) · ★3)
